Correct gradient function:

def _gradient_lse(p, w, beta? actually w normalized):
    n=p.size
    N = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    wf = np.asarray(w[::-1], dtype=float)  # flipped symmetric? length 2n-1
    fp = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, N)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(fp, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, N)
    # need h[2n-2 : n-1 : -1]
    g = 2.0 * h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return g

Check length: N at least len(wf)+n-1 = 3n-2. next_pow2(3n-2) (if 3n-2=1? n=1). Good.

Could precompute N for optimizer to reduce.

However w length 2n-1 can have small negative due numerical? weights nonnegative. OK.

Alternative efficient gradient using c weights and p via direct if sparse:
def _grad_direct(p,w,tol):
 n; g=zeros; threshold=w.max()*1e-6; active=np.where(w>threshold)[0]; for k in active: wk=w[k]; i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k); partner=k-np.arange(i0,i1+1); g[i0:i1+1] += wk * p[partner]
 g *=2
This is O(n*active). If active < 20, good. For n=4096, 20*4096=81920 operations in Python loops? The inner uses numpy vector addition 20 times; okay. If active 100, 400k maybe okay. We can choose threshold to limit active. But for early β large active large, use FFT. Implement hybrid: count active with w > wmax*1e-4 maybe. If active < max(16, n//256)? use direct else FFT.

But direct with many active uses Python for loop over active and vector slices; could be slower than FFT for active > ~32. Use FFT default.

def _smooth_max_weights(c, beta):
 m=c.max(); x=(c-m)/beta; ex=np.exp(x-clipped); s=ex.sum(); w=ex/s; return m,w

def _adam_logit(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, beta_frac=0.15, beta_decay=0.985, no_imp_limit=500, z_noise=None, clip_z=50):
    p=normalize p0; n=p.size; Nfull=next_pow2(2n-1); Ngrad=next_pow2(3n-2)
    z=log(p); z-=mean; m,v=...; t=0
    c=full_convolve(p); maxc=c.max; beta=beta_frac*maxc
    best=p.copy; best_score=score; no=0; base_lr=lr
    while time < deadline:
        mval,w=smooth_weights(c,beta)
        g=grad(p,w)
        # numeric check
        uz = p*(g - dot(p,g))
        if not finite: fallback
        t+=1; Adam update: m_hat, v_hat; z_new = z - lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps)
        z_new = clip; z_new -= mean (optional to fix scale; softmax invariant to constant, mean subtract stabilizes)
        p_new=softmax(z_new)
        c_new=full_convolve(p_new); maxc_new=max; score_new=2n*maxc
        if finite and score_new < best_score - 1e-13: update best; no=0
        else: no+=1; if no==? reduce lr after certain.
        # If score_new worse than current by large? We can still accept? Need maintain p for gradient. We can accept all finite steps? Adam with lr maybe jumps. To stabilize, if score_new > current_score * (1+0.05) or > best? Maybe reject and shrink lr.
        # Simpler: always accept if finite; but can oscillate. Use line search: proposed z_new; compute score_new; if score_new > current_score + 1e-3? shrink. But current_score might not be best. We can maintain a runtime p and score. If rejected, z remains, lr*0.5.
        # We need current_score from p. Let's do: f_cur = score(p). If f_new <= f_cur + 1e-12*max(1,f_cur): accept (non-increase tolerance); else reject, lr=max(min_lr,lr*0.5), keep z, reset Adam? Maybe not.
        # Since Adam direction approximate, accepting modest increases can escape. Use tolerance 1e-4 relative.
        # Also periodically (every 200 steps) if no accept, restart.
        beta update every X: beta=max(beta_min, beta*beta_decay); if gap small, beta smaller? Maybe.
        if no > no_imp_limit: restart with noise around best.
    return best

Let's test gradient correctness with a tiny example conceptually to avoid sign errors. Use n=3, p=[a,b,c]. c0=a^2, c1=2ab, c2=2ac+b^2, c3=2bc, c4=c^2. Let w. Gradient g0 = ∂/∂a: 2a w0 + 2b w1 + 2? term c2 has 2ac derivative wrt a =2c; w2; no w? c3 no. So g0=2(w0 a + w1 b + w2 c). Our formula s0=∑_k w_k p_{k}: k0 p0 a, k1 p1 b, k2 p2 c => w0a+w1b+w2c. Good. g1: ∂ wrt b: c1 2a w1 + c2 2b w2 + c3 2c w3 =>2(w1 a + w2 b + w3 c). Formula s1=∑_k w_k p_{k-1}: k1 p0 a, k2 p1 b, k3 p2 c. Good. g2=2(w2 a+w3 b+w4 c). Great. So gradient function correct.

Now natural gradient: gradz = p*(g - dot(p,g)). For uniform p and symmetric w? If w flat over k, g_i constant? s_i =∑_{k} w p_{k-i}; if w constant and p constant with boundaries? For k range 0..2n-2, ∑ w_{i+j} p_j; w constant, sum over j valid = p * (number of k for i). Number varies at edges: for i near 0, k=0..n-1+n-1? Actually count n always? For i=0, k=0..n-1? p_{k} valid k0..n-1 count n; also k? k can up to 2n-2, p_{k} valid only 0..n-1, so count n. For i=n-1, k=n-1..2n-2 count n. So constant. Good. g constant, gradz zero.

If w peaked at center k=n-1, s_i = w p_{n-1-i}, so g high for i with large mirrored p. This will reduce mass at positions whose mirror has mass. To reduce center c, yes.

Could use exact score not LSE; direction from w smooth. Good.

Multiplicative gradient line search function:
```
def _natural_step_opt(p, c, beta, eta):
  w=weights; g=grad; gradz=p*(g-dot); scale=rms(gradz); z_new=z - eta*gradz/(scale+eps)
```
But if eta too large, positivity? With z clip and softmax, okay.

Adam may be better. We can also add "softmax temperature" on p itself? We optimize z.

Could use "hedge" algorithm: maintain exponent E_i, update E_i -= η * (local gradient per mass?) Another heuristic: p_i <- p_i / (1 + η g_i/mean) normalize. This is multiplicative with inverse. Equivalent to exp(-η log(1+...)). For small same. Might be stable. We can implement one method.

Let's design `_adam_logit` robust with line search using current score.
Pseudo detailed:
```
def _adam_opt(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, min_lr=1e-4, beta_frac=0.25, beta_decay=0.985, beta_min=1e-4, no_imp_limit=400, noise_scale=None, clip=40.0, accept_tol=1e-5):
    p = normalize(p0)
    n = p.size
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-30)); z -= z.mean()
    m = zeros; v=zeros; t=0
    c = full_convolve(p); maxc=c.max(); beta=max(1e-6, beta_frac*maxc)
    cur_score = 2*n*maxc
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=cur_score; no_improve=0; rejected=0
    base_lr=lr
    while time < deadline:
       # maybe compute fresh c if needed
       mval,w = smooth_max_weights(c,beta)
       g = gradient(p,w)
       if not finite: restart
       gz = p*(g - np.dot(p,g))
       # Adam update
       t+=1
       m = 0.9*m+0.1*gz
       v=0.999*v+0.001*gz*gz
       mh=m/(1-0.9**t); vh=v/(1-0.999**t)
       step = lr * mh/(sqrt(vh)+1e-8)
       # optional limit step norm to avoid huge: max_step = 0.5? step norm? Use element clip.
       step = np.clip(step, -1.0, 1.0) maybe.
       z_new = z - step
       z_new = np.clip(z_new, -clip, clip); z_new -= z_new.mean()
       p_new = softmax(z_new)
       c_new = full_convolve(p_new); score_new=2*n*max
       if finite:
          if score_new <= cur_score + accept_tol*max(1.0,cur_score):
             # accept
             z=p_new? z_new; p=p_new; c=c_new; cur_score=score_new; rejected=0
             if score_new < best_score - 1e-12: best..., no_improve=0
             else: no_improve +=1
          else:
             rejected +=1
             lr = max(min_lr, lr*0.7)
             # keep z,p
             if rejected > 20: restart
       else: restart
       # beta update every 25
       if t % 25 ==0:
          beta = max(beta_min, beta*beta_decay)
          # adjust based on top gap
          if c.size>1:
             idx=np.argsort(c)[::-1]; gap=c[idx[0]]-c[idx[1]]
             if gap < beta*0.1: beta=max(beta_min,beta*0.8)
       # if no_improve > limit or rejected>..., restart from best with noise
       if no_improve > no_imp_limit or rejected > 50:
          ns = noise_scale if not None else choose based on best_score and n (e.g., 0.05)
          z = best_z + ns*rng.normal(size=n)
          p=softmax(z); c=full; cur_score=...
          m.fill(0); v.fill(0); t=0; lr=base_lr; beta=max(1e-3,beta_frac*maxc); no_improve=0; rejected=0
    return best_p,best_score
```

But using accept tolerance with current score non-monotonic: if accepted a slightly worse score, cur_score increases; best remains. This can escape. However no_improve counts steps without best; if we keep accepting worse, may wander. Use if score_new < best_score improvement resets; else no_improve++. If no_improve huge restart.

Let's define helper `_read_parent()`:
```
def _read_parent():
    raw = globals().get('height_sequence_1')
    if raw is None: return None
    arr=np.asarray(raw,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s>1e-6: return arr/s
    return None
```

However Adam with momentum can make line search tricky. We can simply use plain normalized natural gradient without momentum, with backtracking. This may be more reliable. Let's design `_natural_search`:
```
def _natural_search(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.1, max_lr=0.5, min_lr=1e-4, beta_frac=0.3, beta_decay=0.99, no_imp_lim=500, noise=None):
 p,z; c,score; beta
 while time:
   w=weights; g=grad; gradz=p*(g-dot); scale=np.sqrt(mean(gradz^2))+eps; step=lr*gradz/scale
   z_new=z-step; p_new=softmax; score_new
   if score_new <= score + 1e-12: accept; score=score_new; if improve best no=0 else no+1; lr=min(max_lr, lr*1.02)
   else: lr=max(min_lr, lr*0.7); if lr==min and reject_count>10: restart
   beta update
   if no>limit: restart
```
But if lr too high initially, many rejects; okay. This is simple and no Adam state. Does natural gradient direction always descent for small lr? Yes for smooth objective in z? The gradient of f(z)=f(softmax(z)) is gradz. Line search on z should reduce. Our gradz computed as p*(g-dot) is exact gradient of f(p) w.r.t z (assuming g is gradient w.r.t p). If we use LSE? We compute g from smooth weights, so f_smooth = LSE, gradient exact for smooth. But we line search on exact score (max), not LSE. LSE direction may not decrease exact if β large. If β small, LSE approximates max and direction likely descent. Start β moderately; perhaps use LSE value for line search? If minimizing LSE, it's smooth and we can line search on LSE. Then exact score may also improve eventually. We can track both. We can set β small enough (0.05*maxc) so LSE close max. Then exact line search okay. Or compute LSE during weight step and use it for acceptance? The final objective is max, but LSE is surrogate. Using LSE line search ensures surrogate decreases; exact may fluctuate. We can accept if LSE decreases, update best by exact score. But LSE with fixed β may decrease while max increases if other peaks rise. However if β small, okay. We can choose accept based on exact score for safety.

Implement `_gradient_search` with LSE line search:
- Maintain p,z,lse_cur, score_cur.
- Each iter compute w and lse_cur (using c). Compute g, gradz, norm.

Pseudo with exact score:
```
def _gradient_search(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.1, max_lr=0.3, min_lr=1e-4, beta_frac=0.2, beta_decay=0.995, beta_min=1e-3? relative, no_imp_limit=400, noise_scale=None, clip=40):
    p=normalize; n; z=log(p)-mean
    c=full; score=...
    beta=max(1e-8, beta_frac*score/(2n)? maxc)
    best=p.copy; best_score=score; no=0; rejects=0; steps=0
    base_lr=lr
    while time<deadline:
       mval,w=smooth_weights(c,beta)
       g=grad(p,w)
       if not finite: restart
       gz=p*(g-np.dot(p,g))
       scale=np.sqrt(np.mean(gz*gz))+1e-15
       step=lr*gz/scale
       # limit step norm? max element clip 2.0
       step=np.clip(step,-2.0,2.0)
       for try in range(8):
          z_new=z - step
          z_new=clip; z_new-=mean
          p_new=softmax(z_new)
          c_new=full; score_new=score
          if finite and score_new <= score + 1e-12 + 1e-12*score:
             accept=True; break
          step *=0.5
       if accept:
          z,p,c,score=z_new,...; rejects=0
          if score < best_score -1e-12: best, no=0 else no+=1
          # adapt lr up occasionally
          if no==0: lr=min(max_lr, lr*1.05)
       else:
          rejects+=1; lr=max(min_lr, lr*0.7)
          if rejects>20: restart
       steps+=1
       if steps % 20 ==0:
          beta=max(beta_min_abs, beta*beta_decay) # beta_min_abs maybe 0.001*maxc? Actually beta relative to current max; use beta_frac_cur * maxc? Better store beta_frac and update.
```

Better maintain `beta_frac` and recompute `beta = beta_frac * maxc` each iter. Decay beta_frac: `beta_frac = max(beta_frac_min, beta_frac*beta_frac_decay)`. Then if maxc changes, beta scales. beta_frac_min maybe 0.01.

Line search inner tries recompute full convolve multiple times; expensive. We can limit tries 5. If lr small, usually accept. Maybe use single step and reduce lr if reject (no inner loop) to save. But line search robust. Use max 6.

If exact score line search too strict due to flat regions, no improvement but score equal. Accept equal (tolerance). If many equals, no_improve increments and restart. Could be okay.

Gradient direct:
```
def _gradient_direct(p,w,active_thr_frac=1e-4):
    n=p.size; wmax=w.max(); tol=wmax*active_thr_frac
    active=np.where(w>tol)[0]
    g=np.zeros(n)
    for k in active:
       wk=w[k]
       i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
       # partner indices: k-i for i in i0..i1 => from k-i0 down to k-i1
       partner_vals = p[i0:i1+1]? Need p[k-i]. Use slice reversed:
       # i array = np.arange(i0,i1+1); partner = k - i = (k-i0) down to (k-i1)
       # This is p[k-i0 : k-i1-1 : -1] if k-i0 <= n-1 and k-i1 >=0, true.
       g[i0:i1+1] += wk * p[k-i0 : k-i1-1 : -1]
    return 2*g
```
Need careful when i1==i0: slice `p[k-i0 : k-i0 : -1]` empty? In Python slice with start==stop and step -1 yields empty. Better use if length 1: g[i0] += wk*p[k-i0]. Or use `idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1); g[i0:i1+1] += wk * p[k-idx]`. For active few, creating idx each loop okay but allocate. Could precompute? Simpler use idx for correctness. For n_hot <= 32, idx length up to n, but active few; memory okay. Use if length large? If active=1, idx length ~n, one allocation, fine. For active many, use FFT.

Threshold: if number active `len(active) <= max(8, n//512)` use direct. For n=4096, n//512=8; active <=8. For early beta large active maybe >8, use FFT. As beta decays, direct cheaper.

```
wf = w[::-1]

```

```
def _full_convolve(a,N=None):
 n=a.size
 if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
 fa=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(fa*fa,N); return c[:2*n-1]
```

```
def _smooth_weights(c,beta):
 c=np.asarray(c,float)
 m=float(c.max())
 if beta <= 1e-15:
    w=np.zeros_like(c); mask=c >= m - 1e-12*max(1,m); if mask sum 0: w[argmax]=1; else w[mask]=1/mask.sum(); return m,w
 x=(c-m)/beta
 # To avoid overflow, x <=0; but if c-m positive? m max so <=0. if numerical >0, clip 0.
 x=np.minimum(x,0.0)
 x=np.maximum(x,-50.0) # exp underflow okay; but if beta very small, x can -1e-13? no.
 ex=np.exp(x)
 s=ex.sum()
 if not finite or s<=0: onehot
 w=ex/s
 return m,w
```

FFT gradient: We can precompute Ngrad in optimizer and pass to function to avoid next_pow2 each time.
```
def _gradient_fft(p,w,Ngrad):
   wf=w[::-1]
   Fw=np.fft.rfft(wf,Ngrad)
   Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,Ngrad)
   h=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fp,Ngrad)
   return 2.0*h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
Potential memory: `h` length Ngrad; for n=8192 Ngrad=32768, fine.

Detailed `_gradient_search` implementation:

```
def _gradient_search(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.12, max_lr=0.35, min_lr=1e-4,
                    beta_frac=0.35, beta_frac_min=0.02, beta_decay=0.997,
                    no_imp_limit=600, noise_scale=None, clip=40.0, tag=""):
    p = _normalize_nonneg(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0: return p, inf
    Nfull=_next_pow2(2*n-1); Ngrad=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z -= z.mean()
    c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
    score = 2*n*float(c.max())
    best_p=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=score
    beta_frac_cur=beta_frac
    no=0; rejects=0; steps=0; restarts=0
    base_lr=lr; noise_auto = noise_scale is None
    while time < deadline:
        if score >= best_score - 1e-12:
            # current maybe not best; okay
            pass
        mval,w = _smooth_weights(c, beta_frac_cur * max(score/(2*n), 1e-12))
        g = _gradient(p,w,Ngrad,n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            # fallback
            z=best_z.copy(); p=best_p.copy(); c=_full_convolve(p,Nfull); score=2*n*max; beta_frac_cur=max(0.2,beta_frac_cur); continue
        gz = p * (g - float(np.dot(p,g)))
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gz*gz)))
        if not finite or scale<1e-18:
            # no direction, restart
            ...
        step = lr * gz / scale
        step = np.clip(step, -2.0, 2.0)
        accepted=False
        step_trial = step.copy()
        for _ in range(5):
            z_t = z - step_trial
            z_t = np.clip(z_t, -clip, clip)
            z_t -= float(np.mean(zt))
            p_t = _softmax_from_z(z_t)
            c_t = _full_convolve(p_t, Nfull)
            score_t = 2*n*float(c_t.max())
            if np.isfinite(score_t) and score_t <= score + 1e-10:
                accepted=True; break
            step_trial *= 0.5
        if accepted:
            z,p,c,score = z_t,p_t,c_t,score_t
            rejects=0
            # maybe increase lr if improving or no reject
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p=p.copy(); best_z=z.copy(); best_score=score
                no=0
                if not quiet: print maybe every 100 improvements
            else:
                no += 1
            lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.03)
        else:
            rejects += 1
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.7)
            beta_frac_cur = min(0.8, beta_frac_cur * 1.2)  # smoother
            if rejects > 15 or no > no_imp_limit:
                # restart
                restarts +=1
                if noise_auto:
                    if best_score < 1.52: ns=0.05
                    elif best_score < 1.56: ns=0.12
                    else: ns=0.25
                else: ns=noise_scale
                if restarts > 1 and ns==0? ensure >0.01
                z = best_z + ns * rng.normal(size=n)
                z = np.clip(z,-clip,clip); z-=mean
                p = _softmax_from_z(z)
                c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
                score = 2*n*max
                beta_frac_cur = max(0.15, beta_frac_cur)
                lr = base_lr
                no=0; rejects=0
        steps +=1
        if steps % 25 ==0:
            beta_frac_cur = max(beta_frac_min, beta_frac_cur * beta_decay)
            # If top coefficients are very flat relative to beta, decay faster? 
            if c.size >=2:
                top = np.sort(c)[::-1][:2]
                gap = top[0]-top[1]
                beta = beta_frac_cur * (score/(2*n))
                if gap < beta*0.1:
                    beta_frac_cur = max(beta_frac_min, beta_frac_cur*0.9)
        # print every 1000 steps?
    return best_p,best_score
```

Coordinate descent can be more effective than global gradient. Let's design a better coordinate/local optimizer to polish.

Local pair move:
Given p, c, peak k. The peak c_k = ∑_{i=i0}^{i1} p_i p_{k-i}. To reduce it, we can move mass from a contributing pair (i,j) to some (r,s) such that other peaks don't increase. Simple move: choose i with large contribution to multiple high peaks, reduce p_i and increase p_j. Gradient sign indicates which positions to increase/decrease. Gradient search already does this globally.

But coordinate descent with exact line search on two coordinates can fine-tune. For n=1024, we can do many. Implementation:
- For target length, maintain p.
- Compute c.
- Choose top few peak lags (e.g., 5). For each, find the pair (i,j) with largest product p_i p_{k-i}. Let i (larger mass) reduce, choose target t that minimizes increase in peaks (maybe a random position not in partner sets of top peaks). Move small fraction δ.
- Use line search over δ (0 to min(p_i, etc.)) to minimize max c exactly (or smooth). This is like multi-start local. But may be slow and not beat gradient.

Could use scipy `optimize.minimize_scalar` on δ for candidate moves. Too much.

Maybe use "spectral" method: The objective max c. The gradient direction is in span. Adam/gradient likely sufficient.

LP refinement function:
```
def _lp_refine(a, deadline, t=0.3, max_solves=25, m_factor=1.02, beta? no):
    a=normalize; c=full; M=c.max(); score=2n M
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    target = best_score/(2n)
```

```
def _adam_search(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.05, max_lr=0.2, beta_frac=0.3, beta_decay=0.99, no_imp_limit=500, noise=0.1, clip=40):
  p,z; m,v,t; c,score; best...
  while time:
    w=stats; g=grad; gz=p*(g-dot)
    t++; m=...; v=...
    step=lr*mh/(sqrt(vh)+eps); clip
    z_new=z-step; p_new=softmax; c_new; score_new
    if finite:
       p=z_new? accept unconditionally? If score_new < best update; else no++
       if score_new > score * (1+0.1) + 1e-6: # too bad, revert
           z,p,c,score=previous; lr*=0.5
       else current update
    beta update, restart if no>limit
```

Now, direct gradient with active threshold: If weights one-hot, active size 1, direct. Good.

Let's code `_gradient` carefully:
```
def _gradient(p, w, Ngrad):
    n = p.size
    wmax = float(w.max())
    if wmax <= 0: return zeros
    tol = wmax * 1e-6
    active = np.where(w > tol)[0]
    if active.size <= max(8, n // 512):
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in active:
            wk = float(w[k])
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1)
            g[i0:i1 + 1] += wk * p[k - idx]
        return 2.0 * g
    else:
        wf = w[::-1]
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Ngrad)
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, Ngrad)
        h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, Ngrad)
        return 2.0 * h[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
```
When `k-idx` valid? For i0=max(0,k-n+1), i1=min(n-1,k). For i in [i0,i1], k-i between k-i1 and k-i0. k-i1 >= k-(n-1?) If i1=k when k<n, k-i1=0. If i1=n-1, k-i1 = k-n+1 = i0? nonnegative. k-i0 <=? if i0=0, k <=n-1 maybe if k<n; if k>=n, i0=k-n+1, k-i0=n-1. Valid. Good.

Now, line search in `_gradient_search` uses `_full_convolve(p_t, Nfull)`. To speed, maybe reuse Fp? Not.

Now, let's improve LP building with `scipy.sparse.diags`. Need import `csr_matrix`, `diags`. We already import csr_matrix; add `from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags`. Use `diags` in `_lp_solve`.
```
def _lp_solve(a, M, time_limit=30):
    n=a.size
    if n ==0: return None
    data=[]
    offsets=np.arange(n, dtype=int)
    for d in range(n):
        data.append(np.full(n-d, a[d], dtype=np.float64))
    A = diags(data, offsets=offsets, shape=(2*n-1, n), format='csr')
    c_obj=-np.ones(n)
    b_ub=M*np.ones(2*n-1)
    bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': float(time_limit)})
    if res.success: return np.clip(res.x,0,None)
    return None
```
Is `diags` with positive offsets and data lengths n-d correct? Let's test n=3. data d0 len3 values a0 offset0: rows0,1,2 cols0,1,2. d1 len2 offset1: rows1,2 cols0,1. d2 len1 offset2: row2 col0. A rows0 col0=a0; row1 col0=a1, col1=a0; row2 col0=a2, col1=a1, col2=a0. This matches A[k,i]=a_{k-i} for k row, i col: k=1,i=0 a1; k=1,i=1 a0. Good. Row2 col0 a2, col1 a1, col2 a0. Good.
Memory: `data` list of arrays; after constructing diags, data still referenced until function ends. Fine. Could build in chunks? no.

For n=4096, data list 4096 arrays total ~8M floats; okay but high. We'll restrict LP n<=2048.

```
def _lp_refine(a, deadline, t=0.4, max_solves=20, tag=""):

    target=best_score/(2n)
    solves=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves < max_solves:

        # maybe solve if M_use > target*1.02 else target*1.02
        if M_use < target * 1.005:
            M_use = target * 1.005
        b=_lp_solve(a, M_use, time_limit=25)
        solves +=1
        if b is None: continue
        b=np.clip(b,0,None)
        s=float(b.sum())
        if s < 1e-12: continue
        b=b/s
        new=(1-t)*a + t*b
        new=_normalize_nonneg(new)
        cnew=_full_convolve(new); snew=2n*max
        if np.isfinite(snew) and snew < best_score -1e-12:
             best_score=snew; best=new.copy()
        # update current a: if new score close or better take new, else maybe keep a but try smaller t? 
        if snew < float(c.max())? use snew < score + 1e-9: score=snew; a=new; c=cnew
        else:
             # try half t?
             new2=a + 0.5*(b-a); new2=norm; sc2=score
             if sc2 < best_score: update
             if sc2 < score: a=new2; c=full; score=sc2
        # adapt t
        t=max(0.15,t*0.92)
        M_use for next? uses c max
    return best,best_score
```

```
def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a, 1e-15, 1000.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if not finite or s <= 0: return np.ones_like(a)/a.size
    return a/s
```

Detailed code draft:

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags

def _next_pow2(n):
    k=1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)

def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0])
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-15, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s

def _full_convolve(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    # remove tiny negative
    c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)
    return c

def _score_norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float('inf')
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    c = _full_convolve(a)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float('inf')
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)
```
Note `_score_norm` normalizes, maybe changes if called on unnormalized. Good.

`_smooth_stats`:
```
def _smooth_stats(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(c.max())
    if c.size == 0:
        return m, np.array([1.0]), m
    if beta <= 1e-18:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return m, w, m
    x = (c - m) / beta
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return m,w,m
    w = ex / s
    lse = m + beta * float(np.log(s))
    return m, w, lse
```

Gradient:
```
def _gradient(p, w, Ngrad):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    wmax = float(w.max())
    if wmax <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(p)
    tol = wmax * 1e-7
    active = np.where(w > tol)[0]
    max_direct = max(8, n // 256)
    if active.size <= max_direct:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in active:
            wk = float(w[k])
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i0 <= i1:
                idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                g[i0:i1 + 1] += wk * p[k - idx]
        return 2.0 * g
    else:
        wf = w[::-1]
        Fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Ngrad)
        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, Ngrad)
        h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, Ngrad)
        return 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
```
`max_direct` n//256 for n=8192 ->32; direct 32 active may be okay? 32*8192=262k operations, okay. But each active alloc idx length up to n. 32 allocations maybe okay. For early beta active >32 FFT. Good.

`_softmax_from_z`:
```
def _softmax_from_z(z, floor=1e-12):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    p = e + float(floor)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones_like(e) / float(e.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p
```

`_gradient_search`:
Need parameters. Let's write robust.
```
def _gradient_search(p0, deadline, rng, lr=0.12, max_lr=0.4, min_lr=1e-4,
                    beta_frac=0.35, beta_frac_min=0.02, beta_decay=0.997,
                    no_imp_limit=600, noise_scale=None, clip=40.0, momentum=0.0, tag=""):
    p = _normalize_nonneg(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float('inf')
    Nfull = _next_pow2(2*n - 1)
    Ngrad = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z -= float(np.mean(z))
    c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
    score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
    best_p = p.copy(); best_z = z.copy(); best_score = score
    beta_frac_cur = beta_frac
    no = 0; rejects = 0; steps = 0; restarts = 0
    base_lr = lr
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    print_interval = 5000
    if tag:
        print(f"  start {tag}: n={n} score={score:.10f}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        maxc = score / (2.0*n)
        beta = max(1e-18, beta_frac_cur * maxc)
        mval, w, lse_cur = _smooth_stats(c, beta)
        g = _gradient(p, w, Ngrad)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            z = best_z.copy(); p = best_p.copy(); c = _full_convolve(p,Nfull); score = 2*n*float(c.max()); continue
        dot = float(np.dot(p, g))
        gz = p * (g - dot)
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gz*gz)))
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-30:
            # no useful direction
            if restarts < 20:
                # small random perturbation
                z = z + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
                z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip); z -= float(np.mean(z))
                p = _softmax_from_z(z)
                c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull); score=2*n*float(c.max())
            else:
                break
            no += 1
            steps += 1
            continue
        if momentum > 0.0:
            mom = momentum * mom + (1.0 - momentum) * (gz / scale)
            direction = mom
            dirscale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction*direction)))
            if dirscale > 1e-30:
                direction = direction / dirscale
            else:
                direction = gz / scale
        else:
            direction = gz / scale
        step = lr * direction
        step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)
        accepted = False
        step_trial = step.copy()
        z_t = p_t = c_t = None
        score_t = float('inf')
        lse_t = 0.0
        for _ in range(5):
            z_t = z - step_trial
            z_t = np.where(np.isfinite(z_t), z_t, z)
            z_t = np.clip(z_t, -clip, clip)
            z_t -= float(np.mean(z_t))
            p_t = _softmax_from_z(z_t)
            c_t = _full_convolve(p_t, Nfull)
            score_t = 2.0 * n * float(c_t.max())
            # lse_t only if needed? compute cheap
            mval_t, _, lse_t = _smooth_stats(c_t, beta)
            score_tol = 1e-10 * max(1.0, score)
            best_tol = 1e-6 * max(1.0, best_score)
            if np.isfinite(score_t) and (score_t <= score + score_tol or (score_t <= best_score + best_tol and lse_t <= lse_cur + 1e-12)):
                accepted = True
                break
            step_trial *= 0.5
        if accepted:
            z, p, c, score = z_t, p_t, c_t, score_t
            rejects = 0
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = p.copy(); best_z = z.copy(); best_score = score
                no = 0
                if tag and (restarts == 0 and no == 0 and steps % 20 == 0):
                    # not useful
                    pass
            else:
                no += 1
            lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.02)
        else:
            rejects += 1
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.7)
            beta_frac_cur = min(0.8, beta_frac_cur * 1.15)
            if rejects > 20 or no > no_imp_limit:
                restarts += 1
                if noise_scale is None:
                    if best_score < 1.505:
                        ns = 0.03
                    elif best_score < 1.51:
                        ns = 0.06
                    elif best_score < 1.53:
                        ns = 0.12
                    else:
                        ns = 0.25
                else:
                    ns = float(noise_scale)
                if restarts > 1:
                    ns = max(0.02, ns * 0.8)
                z = best_z + ns * rng.normal(size=n)
                z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                p = _softmax_from_z(z)
                c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
                score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
                lr = base_lr
                beta_frac_cur = max(0.15, beta_frac_cur)
                mom.fill(0.0)
                no = 0
                rejects = 0
                if tag:
                    print(f"  {tag} restart {restarts} n={n} best={best_score:.10f}")
        steps += 1
        if steps % 25 == 0:
            beta_frac_cur = max(beta_frac_min, beta_frac_cur * beta_decay)
        if tag and steps % 5000 == 0:
            print(f"  {tag} step {steps} time {time.monotonic()-...}")
    return best_p, best_score
```
Need avoid using `time.monotonic()-...` without t0. We can pass `t_start`? Or omit step print time. Use global? Could print only best score. To show progress, we can print at restart. At start and end. No need step prints (can be many). But user wants output. We can print at function start/end.

Surrogate condition uses `lse_t <= lse_cur + 1e-12`. But `lse` scale ~maxc ~1e-4, 1e-12 absolute is very tight. Use relative: `+ 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(lse_cur))` => 1e-12. Fine.

But computing lse_t in line search loop adds exp O(2n) per try. Maybe okay. Could skip surrogate to save. But keep.

Potential issue: If accepted due to surrogate with score_t slightly > score, current score may increase. Then no increments (not best). It can wander. But best_tol small. Good.

Line search uses current score, not best. If current score is much worse than best (after some accepted surrogate increments?), exact condition may reject improvements that lower current but still >best? If score_t < current, accepted. Good.

What if current score is worse and line search tries step_trial that reduces score but still > current + tol? It will reduce step until maybe score_t <= current, accepted eventually if direction descent. If step too large and all reductions still increase current (because direction bad), reject and restart.

Now, profile functions:
```
def _make_linear(n, slope):
    if n <=1: return np.ones(1)
    t = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    a = 1.0 + slope*(t-0.5)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return a/a.sum()
```
Cubic:
```
def _make_cubic(n,a1,a3):
 t=linspace; u=2t-1; a=1+a1*u+a3*u**3; a=max; normalize
```
Cosine:
```
def _make_cosine(n,freq,amp,phase=0.0):
 t=np.arange(n); a=1+amp*np.cos(2π freq t/n+phase); max
```
Exp profile:
```
def _make_exp_profile(n,a,b):
 t; u=t-0.5; a=np.exp(a*u + b*u*u); max; normalize
```
Random fourier:
```
def _make_random_fourier(n,M,rng,ampl=1.0):
 z=np.zeros(n)
 for m in range(1,M+1):
    amp = ampl/(np.sqrt(m)+0.5)
    ph = rng.uniform(0,2π)
    z += amp*np.cos(2π*m*np.arange(n)/n + ph)
 # maybe add small slope? softmax
 p=_softmax_from_z(z)
 return p
```
But softmax of zero-mean z with amplitude maybe constant-ish. Good.
Blocks:
```
def _make_blocks(n,K,rng):
 K=max(2,min(K,n))
 heights=np.exp(rng.normal(size=K))*0.5+0.1
 a=np.repeat(heights, n//K)
 if len<a n? better use np.tile? 
 edges = np.linspace(0,n,K+1).astype(int)
 for j: a[start:end]=heights[j] * (end-start)? If block heights mean value, not total mass. For profile values within block constant height; sum normalized later. Fine.
 return _normalize_nonneg(a)
```
Simpler: `block_size = n // K; rem = n - block_size*K`; create empty; fill. Or `np.repeat(heights, np.full(K, n//K) + (np.arange(K)<rem))`. Use no lambda. We'll write simple:
```
sizes = np.full(K, n//K, dtype=int); sizes[:n%K]+=1
a = np.repeat(heights, sizes)
```
Good.

Interp:
```
def _interp_profile(p, n):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel(); p=_normalize_nonneg(p)
    n0=p.size
    if n0 == n: return p
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n0)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    q=np.interp(x1, x0, p)
    return _normalize_nonneg(q)
```

Read parent:
```
def _read_parent():
    arr = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if arr is None: return None
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr),arr,0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-6: return None
    return arr/s
```

LP functions as above.

Active cutting-plane LP:
Given a, current c. We want solve max b s.t. A b ≤ M (all 2n-1). We can start with a subset of constraints, e.g., top K lags (or all with c_k > 0.5M). Solve, get b. Check full a*b; if max violation > M*(1+tol), add most violated constraints and resolve. This reduces matrix rows. For n=4096, adding constraints gradually. Each LP with R rows x n. If R ~ thousands, nnz R*n maybe still high but less. Could allow LP at 4096. But implementing robust in time complex. However could significantly improve.

Simpler active LP by using only rows for active peaks? If we omit constraints, b may violate omitted, but if we check and reject if violation too high, maybe useful. We can do:
- Solve LP with rows top R (e.g., 200) and M = target. Get b.
- Blend and compute exact score; if improves, accept.
This is a heuristic, not true constrained. The returned b is good under top constraints. We can solve small LP many times. This may be powerful.

Implement `_lp_active(a, M, row_indices)`: Construct A with selected rows only. For each row k, nonzeros length up to n. If R~200, nnz 200*2048 for n=4096 ~409k, fast. rows_list=[]; cols_list=[]; vals_list=[]

```
A=csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)),shape=(len(rows),n))
```

Add active LP code:
```
def _lp_solve_rows(a, M, rows, time_limit=20.0):
    n=a.size; r=len(rows)
    if r==0: return None
    rowarr=[]; colarr=[]; valarr=[]
    for ridx, k in enumerate(rows):
        i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
        idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1, dtype=np.int64)
        ridxs = [ridx]*len(idx)
        rowarr.extend(ridxs); colarr.extend(idx.tolist()); valarr.extend(a[k-idx].tolist())
    if not rowarr: return None
    A=csr_matrix((np.asarray(valarr), (np.asarray(rowarr), np.asarray(colarr))), shape=(r,n))
    c_obj=-np.ones(n); b_ub=M*np.ones(r); bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n
    try: res=linprog(... options={'time_limit':...})
    except: res=linprog(...)
    if res.success: return np.clip(res.x,0,None)
    return None
```
List extend with `[ridx]*len(idx)` alloc list; okay for R small. Could use `np.full(len(idx), ridx, dtype=int).tolist()`. Fine.

Active refine:
```
def _lp_active_refine(a, deadline, top_k=256, t=0.3, max_solves=30, tag=""):
   a=norm; c=full; M=max; score; best
   target=best/(2n)
   solves=0
   top_k_eff = min(c.size, max(64, min(top_k, c.size//4)))
   while time<deadline and solves<max_solves:
      if c.size > top_k_eff:
          rows = np.argpartition(c, -top_k_eff)[-top_k_eff:]
      else: rows=np.arange(c.size)
      M_use = max(target*1.015, M*0.998)
      b=_lp_solve_rows(a, M_use, rows)
      solves+=1
      if b is None: continue
      s=b.sum(); if s<1e-12: continue
      b/=s
      new=(1-t)*a + t*b
      new=norm; sc=score
      if sc<best_score: update best
      if sc < score + 1e-9:
          a=new; c=full; score=sc; M=max
      else:
          # try half
          new05 = a + 0.5*(b-a); norm; sc05
          if sc05<best_score: update
          if sc05 < score:
             a=new05; c=full; score=sc05; M=max
      t=max(0.1, t*0.95)
   return best
```
Since target based on best, if best improves, target lowers. Good.

Now, maybe add a function `_convolve_ab(a,b)` to check violations and add rows. Could improve active LP. Compute cross-correlation a*b (not square) length 2n-1: c_ab = a*b (convolution). Since a,b nonnegative. This is like FFT of a and b. If max c_ab > M, add most violated rows next solve. This is proper cutting-plane. We can implement easily:
```
def _convolve_ab(a,b,N=None): N=next_pow2(2n-1); fa=rfft(a,N); fb=rfft(b,N); c=irfft(fa*fb,N)[:2n-1]; return max(0,c)
```
Then active LP cutting-plane:
- Start rows top of a*a.
- Solve b.
- Compute violations = convolve_ab(a,b) - M_use. Add top violated rows to active set (maintain list).
- Continue with expanded rows until no violation or max rows limit.
This could solve with fewer rows than full but maintain feasibility. Then blend. This is robust. Let's design `_lp_cutting_refine`:
```
def _lp_cutting(a, deadline, t=0.3, max_rows=1024, max_solves=10, tag=""):
  a=norm; c=full; M=target?; best
  while time:
     target=best/(2n)
     M_use = max(target*1.01, c.max()*0.999?)
     rows = initial top top_min from c
     active_rows = rows
     while True:
        b = _lp_solve_rows(a, M_use, active_rows)
        if b is None: break
        # check violation over full convolve_ab
        ab = _convolve_ab(a,b)
        viol = ab - M_use
        max_viol = ab.max() - M_use
        if max_viol <= 1e-10: feasible break
        # add top violated rows not in active
        # To avoid set, use boolean mask
        if active_rows < max_rows:
           add_k = max(32, (max_rows - active_rows)//2)
           # get top violated rows: where viol > 0
           if np.any(viol > 1e-12):
              add = np.argpartition(viol, -add_k)[-add_k:]
              # combine unique
              active_flags = np.zeros(c.size, bool); active_flags[active_rows]=True; active_flags[add]=True
              active_rows = np.where(active_flags)[0]
              if active_rows.size >= max_rows: # if grew, maybe stop after this solve? 
              continue # resolve with new rows
        else: # at max rows, accept infeasible b? or use top allowed
           break
     if b is None: break
     blend...
```
This can be expensive: multiple LP solves per b. But rows max 512, n=4096, maybe okay. For 1024, max_rows=2047 (full) eventually. This is powerful.

```
"""
Optimizes nonnegative step-function coefficients by minimizing max convolution.
Combines correct natural-gradient line search on softmax parameters, adaptive
smooth-max weighting, multi-scale interpolation, LP best-response refinements,
and diverse restarts.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags
```

```
       choose=parent_clean
```

```
def _lp_solve_rows(a, M, rows, time_limit=20.0):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); n=a.size
    rows=np.asarray(rows,dtype=np.int64)
    r=rows.size
    if r==0 or M <=0: return None
    i0_arr=np.empty(r,dtype=np.int64); i1_arr=np.empty(r); lens=np.empty(r)
    total=0
    for j,k in enumerate(rows):
        k=int(k)
        i0=max(0,k-n+1); i1=min(n-1,k)
        L=i1-i0+1
        i0_arr[j]=i0; i1_arr[j]=i1; lens[j]=L; total+=L
    if total==0: return None
    rows_arr=np.repeat(np.arange(r,dtype=np.int64), lens)
    cols_arr=np.empty(total,dtype=np.int64)
    vals_arr=np.empty(total,dtype=np.float64)
    pos=0
    for j,k in enumerate(rows):
        i0=int(i0_arr[j]); i1=int(i1_arr[j]); L=int(lens[j])
        idx=np.arange(i0,i1+1,dtype=np.int64)
        cols_arr[pos:pos+L]=idx
        vals_arr[pos:pos+L]=a[k-idx]
        pos+=L
    A=csr_matrix((vals_arr,(rows_arr,cols_arr)),shape=(r,n))
    ...
```

```
def _lp_active_refine(a, deadline, rng=None, top_k=256, t=0.35, max_solves=20, tag=""):
   a=norm; n; c; M; score; best; target
   stagnant=0
   for solves in range(max_solves):
      if time>deadline break
      if c.size > top_k:
         if rng is not None and stagnant > 0:
             k1 = max(32, top_k//2)
             top = np.argpartition(c, -k1)[-k1:]
             rand = rng.integers(0, c.size, size=top_k-k1)
             rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, rand)))
         else:
             rows = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
      else: rows=np.arange(c.size)
      M_use = max(target*1.015, M*0.999)
      b = _lp_solve_rows(a, M_use, rows)
      if b is None: stagnant+=1; continue
      s=b.sum(); if s<... stagnant+=1; continue
      b/=s
      new=norm((1-t)*a + t*b); sc=score
      improved=False
      if sc < best_score -1e-12: best...; improved=True
      if sc < score + 1e-9:
          a=new; c=full; score=sc; M=max
          improved=True? maybe not if sc not best but current improved.
      else:
          half=norm(a +0.5*(b-a)); sc2=score
          if sc2 < best_score: best...
          if sc2 < score:
             a=half; c=full; score=sc2; M=max
      if best_score updated: target=best_score/(2n); stagnant=0
      else: stagnant+=1
      t=max(0.12,t*0.95)
      if stagnant > 5: break
   return best
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for nonnegative step-function coefficients.

It minimizes max_k (a*a)_k / (sum a)^2 by:
- using the correct natural-gradient direction for the softmax parameterization,
- adaptive smooth-max weighting / line search,
- multi-scale interpolation to larger lengths,
- LP best-response refinements (full for small n, active/low-rank for larger n),
- diverse restarts and local high-temperature annealing.
"""
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix, diags


def _next_pow2(n):
    k = 1
    while k < n:
        k <<= 1
    return max(2, k)


def _normalize_nonneg(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 1e-15, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _full_convolve(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if N is None:
        N = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    return np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)


def _score_norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    c = _full_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def _smooth_stats(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64), 0.0
    m = float(c.max())
    if beta <= 1e-18 or not np.isfinite(beta):
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w, m
    x = (c - m) / beta
    x = np.minimum(x, 0.0)
    x = np.maximum(x, -745.0)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return m, w, m
    w = ex / s
    lse = m + beta * float(np.log(s))
    if not np.isfinite(lse):
        lse = m
    return m, w, lse


def _gradient(p, w, Ngrad):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    wmax = float(w.max())
    if wmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(wmax):
        return np.zeros_like(p)

    tol = wmax * 1e-7
    active = np.where(w > tol)[0]
    max_direct = max(8, n // 256)

    if active.size <= max_direct:
        g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in active:
            wk = float(w[k])
            i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
            i1 = min(n - 1, k)
            if i0 <= i1:
                idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
                g[i0:i1 + 1] += wk * p[k - idx]
        return 2.0 * g

    wf = w[::-1]
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(wf, Ngrad)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, Ngrad)
    h = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fp, Ngrad)
    return 2.0 * h[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]


def _softmax_from_z(z, floor=1e-12):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    e = np.exp(z)
    p = e + float(floor)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(e.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(e.size)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def _gradient_search(
    p0,
    deadline,
    rng,
    lr=0.12,
    max_lr=0.40,
    min_lr=1e-4,
    beta_frac=0.35,
    beta_frac_min=0.02,
    beta_decay=0.997,
    no_imp_limit=600,
    noise_scale=None,
    clip=40.0,
    momentum=0.0,
    tag=""
):
    p = _normalize_nonneg(p0)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, float("inf")

    Nfull = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Ngrad = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z -= float(np.mean(z))

    c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_score = score

    beta_frac_cur = beta_frac
    no = 0
    rejects = 0
    steps = 0
    restarts = 0
    base_lr = lr
    mom = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if tag:
        print(f"  _gradient_search start tag={tag} n={n} score={score:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        maxc = max(1e-300, score / (2.0 * n))
        beta = max(1e-18, beta_frac_cur * maxc)
        _, w, lse_cur = _smooth_stats(c, beta)
        g = _gradient(p, w, Ngrad)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            z = best_z.copy()
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
            score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
            steps += 1
            continue

        dot = float(np.dot(p, g))
        gz = p * (g - dot)
        scale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gz * gz)))

        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale < 1e-30:
            z = z + 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
            z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
            z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
            z -= float(np.mean(z))
            p = _softmax_from_z(z)
            c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
            score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
            no += 1
            steps += 1
            if no > no_imp_limit:
                restarts += 1
                ns = 0.10 if noise_scale is None else float(noise_scale)
                z = best_z + ns * rng.normal(size=n)
                z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                p = _softmax_from_z(z)
                c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
                score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
                beta_frac_cur = max(0.15, beta_frac_cur)
                lr = base_lr
                mom.fill(0.0)
                no = 0
                rejects = 0
            continue

        if momentum > 0.0:
            mom = momentum * mom + (1.0 - momentum) * (gz / scale)
            direction = mom
            dirscale = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(direction * direction)))
            if not np.isfinite(dirscale) or dirscale < 1e-30:
                direction = gz / scale
            else:
                direction = direction / dirscale
        else:
            direction = gz / scale

        step = lr * direction
        step = np.where(np.isfinite(step), step, 0.0)
        step = np.clip(step, -3.0, 3.0)

        accepted = False
        step_trial = step.copy()
        z_t = z.copy()
        p_t = p.copy()
        c_t = c.copy()
        score_t = score
        lse_t = lse_cur

        score_tol = 1e-10 * max(1.0, score)
        best_tol = 1e-6 * max(1.0, best_score)

        for _ in range(4):
            z_trial = z - step_trial
            z_trial = np.where(np.isfinite(z_trial), z_trial, z)
            z_trial = np.clip(z_trial, -clip, clip)
            z_trial -= float(np.mean(z_trial))

            p_trial = _softmax_from_z(z_trial)
            c_trial = _full_convolve(p_trial, Nfull)
            score_trial = 2.0 * n * float(c_trial.max())
            _, _, lse_trial = _smooth_stats(c_trial, beta)

            ok = np.isfinite(score_trial)
            if ok:
                if score_trial <= score + score_tol:
                    ok = True
                elif score_trial <= best_score + best_tol and lse_trial <= lse_cur + 1e-12:
                    ok = True
                else:
                    ok = False

            if ok:
                z_t = z_trial
                p_t = p_trial
                c_t = c_trial
                score_t = score_trial
                lse_t = lse_trial
                accepted = True
                break

            step_trial *= 0.5

        if accepted:
            z = z_t
            p = p_t
            c = c_t
            score = score_t
            rejects = 0

            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                best_score = score
                no = 0
            else:
                no += 1

            lr = min(max_lr, lr * 1.02)
        else:
            rejects += 1
            lr = max(min_lr, lr * 0.7)
            beta_frac_cur = min(0.80, beta_frac_cur * 1.15)

            if rejects > 20 or no > no_imp_limit:
                restarts += 1
                if noise_scale is None:
                    if best_score < 1.505:
                        ns = 0.03
                    elif best_score < 1.510:
                        ns = 0.06
                    elif best_score < 1.530:
                        ns = 0.12
                    else:
                        ns = 0.25
                else:
                    ns = float(noise_scale)
                if restarts > 1:
                    ns = max(0.02, 0.8 * ns)
                z = best_z + ns * rng.normal(size=n)
                z = np.clip(z, -clip, clip)
                z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
                z -= float(np.mean(z))
                p = _softmax_from_z(z)
                c = _full_convolve(p, Nfull)
                score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max())
                lr = base_lr
                beta_frac_cur = max(0.15, beta_frac_cur)
                mom.fill(0.0)
                no = 0
                rejects = 0
                if tag:
                    print(f"  {tag} restart {restarts} n={n} best={best_score:.10f}")

        steps += 1
        if steps % 25 == 0:
            beta_frac_cur = max(beta_frac_min, beta_frac_cur * beta_decay)

    if tag:
        print(f"  _gradient_search end tag={tag} n={best_p.size} best={best_score:.10f}")
    return best_p, best_score


def _interp_profile(p, n):
    p = _normalize_nonneg(p)
    n0 = p.size
    if n0 == n:
        return p
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = np.interp(x1, x0, p)
    return _normalize_nonneg(q)


def _make_linear(n, slope):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    a = 1.0 + float(slope) * (t - 0.5)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return a / a.sum()


def _make_cubic(n, a1, a3):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = 2.0 * t - 1.0
    a = 1.0 + float(a1) * u + float(a3) * u * u * u
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return a / a.sum()


def _make_cosine(n, freq, amp, phase=0.0):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(freq) * t / float(n) + float(phase))
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return a / a.sum()


def _make_exp_profile(n, a, b):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    u = t - 0.5
    a = np.exp(float(a) * u + float(b) * u * u)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
    return a / a.sum()


def _make_blocks(n, K, rng):
    K = max(2, min(int(K), int(n)))
    heights = np.exp(rng.normal(size=K, scale=0.5)) * 0.6 + 0.2
    sizes = np.full(K, n // K, dtype=np.int64)
    rem = n - K * (n // K)
    if rem > 0:
        sizes[:rem] += 1
    a = np.repeat(heights, sizes)
    return _normalize_nonneg(a)


def _make_random_fourier(n, M, rng):
    if n <= 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    amp0 = 1.0
    for m in range(1, int(M) + 1):
        amp = amp0 / (np.sqrt(float(m)) + 0.5)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * t / float(n) + ph)
    p = _softmax_from_z(z)
    return p


def _read_parent():
    raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if raw is None:
        return None
    arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-6 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return arr / s


def _lp_solve_full(a, M, time_limit=25.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    data = []
    for d in range(n):
        data.append(np.full(n - d, float(a[d]), dtype=np.float64))

    try:
        A = diags(
            data,
            offsets=np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64),
            shape=(2 * n - 1, n),
            format="csr"
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = float(M) * np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res.success:
        return np.where(np.isfinite(res.x), np.maximum(res.x, 0.0), 0.0)
    return None


def _lp_refine_full(a, deadline, t=0.35, max_solves=10, tag=""):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    c = _full_convolve(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    score = 2.0 * n * M
    best_p = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
    stagnant = 0
    solves = 0

    if tag:
        print(f"  _lp_refine_full start n={n} score={score:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves < max_solves:
        M_use = max(target * 1.010, M * 0.999)
        b = _lp_solve_full(a, M_use, time_limit=20.0)
        solves += 1
        if b is None:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break
            continue

        sb = float(b.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sb) or sb < 1e-12:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break
            continue

        b = b / sb
        new = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
        new = _normalize_nonneg(new)
        cnew = _full_convolve(new)
        scnew = 2.0 * n * float(cnew.max())

        improved = False
        if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = new.copy()
            best_score = scnew
            target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            improved = True

        if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < score + 1e-9:
            a = new
            c = cnew
            score = scnew
            M = float(c.max())
            improved = True
        else:
            half = a + 0.5 * (b - a)
            half = _normalize_nonneg(half)
            ch = _full_convolve(half)
            sch = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
            if np.isfinite(sch) and sch < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = half.copy()
                best_score = sch
                target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            if np.isfinite(sch) and sch < score + 1e-9:
                a = half
                c = ch
                score = sch
                M = float(ch.max())
                improved = True

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break

        t = max(0.15, t * 0.95)

    if tag:
        print(f"  _lp_refine_full end n={best_p.size} best={best_score:.10f}")
    return best_p, best_score


def _lp_solve_rows(a, M, rows, time_limit=20.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64).ravel()
    r = rows.size
    if n == 0 or r == 0 or M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    i0_arr = np.empty(r, dtype=np.int64)
    i1_arr = np.empty(r, dtype=np.int64)
    lens = np.empty(r, dtype=np.int64)
    total = 0

    for j in range(r):
        k = int(rows[j])
        i0 = max(0, k - n + 1)
        i1 = min(n - 1, k)
        L = i1 - i0 + 1
        i0_arr[j] = i0
        i1_arr[j] = i1
        lens[j] = L
        total += L

    if total <= 0:
        return None

    row_arr = np.repeat(np.arange(r, dtype=np.int64), lens)
    col_arr = np.empty(total, dtype=np.int64)
    val_arr = np.empty(total, dtype=np.float64)

    pos = 0
    for j in range(r):
        kk = int(rows[j])
        i0 = int(i0_arr[j])
        i1 = int(i1_arr[j])
        L = int(lens[j])
        idx = np.arange(i0, i1 + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        col_arr[pos:pos + L] = idx
        val_arr[pos:pos + L] = a[kk - idx]
        pos += L

    try:
        A = csr_matrix((val_arr, (row_arr, col_arr)), shape=(r, n), dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None

    c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = float(M) * np.ones(r, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c_obj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)}
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res.success:
        return np.where(np.isfinite(res.x), np.maximum(res.x, 0.0), 0.0)
    return None


def _lp_refine_active(a, deadline, rng=None, top_k=256, t=0.35, max_solves=18, tag=""):
    a = _normalize_nonneg(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    c = _full_convolve(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    score = 2.0 * n * M
    best_p = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
    stagnant = 0
    solves = 0
    top_k = max(64, min(int(top_k), c.size))

    if tag:
        print(f"  _lp_refine_active start n={n} top_k={top_k} score={score:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and solves < max_solves:
        if c.size > top_k:
            if rng is not None and stagnant > 0:
                k1 = max(32, top_k // 2)
                top = np.argpartition(c, -k1)[-k1:]
                rnd = rng.integers(0, c.size, size=top_k - k1)
                rows = np.unique(np.concatenate((top, rnd)))
            else:
                rows = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
        else:
            rows = np.arange(c.size)

        rows = rows[rows >= 0]
        rows = rows[rows < c.size]
        if rows.size == 0:
            break

        M_use = max(target * 1.015, M * 0.999)
        b = _lp_solve_rows(a, M_use, rows, time_limit=18.0)
        solves += 1

        if b is None:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break
            continue

        sb = float(b.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sb) or sb < 1e-12:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break
            continue

        b = b / sb
        new = (1.0 - t) * a + t * b
        new = _normalize_nonneg(new)
        cnew = _full_convolve(new)
        scnew = 2.0 * n * float(cnew.max())

        improved = False
        if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_p = new.copy()
            best_score = scnew
            target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            improved = True

        if np.isfinite(scnew) and scnew < score + 1e-9:
            a = new
            c = cnew
            score = scnew
            M = float(c.max())
            improved = True
        else:
            half = a + 0.5 * (b - a)
            half = _normalize_nonneg(half)
            ch = _full_convolve(half)
            sch = 2.0 * n * float(ch.max())
            if np.isfinite(sch) and sch < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_p = half.copy()
                best_score = sch
                target = best_score / (2.0 * n)
            if np.isfinite(sch) and sch < score + 1e-9:
                a = half
                c = ch
                score = sch
                M = float(ch.max())
                improved = True

        if improved:
            stagnant = 0
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 5:
                break

        t = max(0.10, t * 0.95)

    if tag:
        print(f"  _lp_refine_active end n={best_p.size} best={best_score:.10f}")
    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**63 - 1))

    parent = _read_parent()
    if parent is None:
        base = _make_linear(1024, 2.0)
    else:
        base = parent

    best_p = _normalize_nonneg(base)
    best_score = _score_norm(best_p)

    parent_p = _normalize_nonneg(parent) if parent is not None else best_p.copy()
    parent_score = _score_norm(parent_p)

    if parent is not None:
        if np.isfinite(parent_score) and parent_score < best_score:
            best_p = parent_p.copy()
            best_score = parent_score

    print(f"initial best: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")
    print(f"parent best : n={parent_p.size} score={parent_score:.10f}")

    # ---------------- Stage 1: polish parent/base ----------------
    st1_g = t0 + total * 0.10
    st1_end = t0 + total * 0.12

    p1, sc1 = _gradient_search(
        best_p,
        st1_g,
        rng,
        lr=0.12,
        max_lr=0.40,
        beta_frac=0.35,
        beta_frac_min=0.03,
        beta_decay=0.997,
        no_imp_limit=400,
        noise_scale=0.10,
        momentum=0.40,
        tag="stage1"
    )
    if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < best_score:
        best_p = p1
        best_score = sc1
        print(f"  stage1 improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    if best_p.size <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < st1_end:
        pl, scl = _lp_refine_full(best_p, st1_end, t=0.35, max_solves=4, tag="stage1_lp")
        if np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
            best_p = pl
            best_score = scl
            print(f"  stage1 LP improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    # ---------------- Stage 2: n=2048 ----------------
    st2_g = t0 + total * 0.31
    st2_end = t0 + total * 0.34
    n2 = 2048

    init2 = _interp_profile(best_p, n2)
    scinit2 = _score_norm(init2)
    if np.isfinite(scinit2) and scinit2 < best_score:
        best_p = init2
        best_score = scinit2
        print(f"  interpolate to n={n2} improved: score={best_score:.10f}")

    p2, sc2 = _gradient_search(
        init2,
        st2_g,
        rng,
        lr=0.15,
        max_lr=0.45,
        beta_frac=0.40,
        beta_frac_min=0.03,
        beta_decay=0.998,
        no_imp_limit=500,
        noise_scale=0.15,
        momentum=0.40,
        tag="n2048"
    )
    if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
        best_p = p2
        best_score = sc2
        print(f"  stage2 improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    if best_p.size <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < st2_end:
        pl, scl = _lp_refine_full(best_p, st2_end, t=0.30, max_solves=4, tag="stage2_lp")
        if np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
            best_p = pl
            best_score = scl
            print(f"  stage2 LP improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    prev_stage_p = best_p.copy()

    # ---------------- Stage 3: n=4096 ----------------
    st3_end = t0 + total * 0.65
    n3 = 4096

    init3 = _interp_profile(best_p, n3)
    scinit3 = _score_norm(init3)
    if np.isfinite(scinit3) and scinit3 < best_score:
        best_p = init3
        best_score = scinit3
        print(f"  interpolate to n={n3} improved: score={best_score:.10f}")

    p3, sc3 = _gradient_search(
        init3,
        st3_end,
        rng,
        lr=0.15,
        max_lr=0.45,
        beta_frac=0.40,
        beta_frac_min=0.03,
        beta_decay=0.998,
        no_imp_limit=600,
        noise_scale=0.15,
        momentum=0.30,
        tag="n4096"
    )
    if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_score:
        best_p = p3
        best_score = sc3
        print(f"  stage3 improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    if best_p.size <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < st3_end:
        topk = max(128, min(512, best_p.size // 8))
        pl, scl = _lp_refine_active(
            best_p,
            st3_end,
            rng,
            top_k=topk,
            t=0.25,
            max_solves=6,
            tag="stage3_active_lp"
        )
        if np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
            best_p = pl
            best_score = scl
            print(f"  stage3 active LP improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    # ---------------- Stage 4: larger scale / alternatives ----------------
    st4_end = t0 + total * 0.84

    if best_p.size <= 4096 and time.monotonic() < st4_end - 80:
        n8 = 8192
        init8 = _interp_profile(best_p, n8)
        scinit8 = _score_norm(init8)
        if np.isfinite(scinit8) and scinit8 < best_score:
            best_p = init8
            best_score = scinit8
            print(f"  interpolate to n={n8} improved: score={best_score:.10f}")

        per8 = min(160.0, max(0.0, st4_end - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        if per8 > 40.0:
            p8, sc8 = _gradient_search(
                init8,
                time.monotonic() + per8,
                rng,
                lr=0.09,
                max_lr=0.35,
                beta_frac=0.25,
                beta_frac_min=0.02,
                beta_decay=0.999,
                no_imp_limit=400,
                noise_scale=0.10,
                momentum=0.20,
                tag="n8192"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc8) and sc8 < best_score:
                best_p = p8
                best_score = sc8
                print(f"  large scale improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    if time.monotonic() < st4_end - 30:
        if best_p.size <= 2048:
            n_alt = int(best_p.size)
        elif parent is not None and parent_p.size <= 2048:
            n_alt = int(parent_p.size)
        else:
            n_alt = 1024

        alt_inits = []
        if parent is not None:
            alt_inits.append(_interp_profile(parent_p, n_alt))
        alt_inits.append(_interp_profile(best_p, n_alt))
        alt_inits.append(_make_linear(n_alt, 3.0))
        alt_inits.append(_make_cubic(n_alt, 2.5, -2.0))
        alt_inits.append(_make_exp_profile(n_alt, -2.0, 1.5))
        alt_inits.append(_make_blocks(n_alt, 10, rng))
        alt_inits.append(_make_random_fourier(n_alt, 8, rng))

        scored = []
        for i, a in enumerate(alt_inits):
            sc = _score_norm(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, i))
        scored.sort()

        for rank, (sc_i, idx) in enumerate(scored[:2]):
            if time.monotonic() > st4_end - 25:
                break
            per_alt = min(70.0, max(0.0, st4_end - time.monotonic() - 15.0))
            if per_alt < 25.0:
                break
            pa = alt_inits[idx]
            p_alt, sc_alt = _gradient_search(
                pa,
                time.monotonic() + per_alt,
                rng,
                lr=0.12,
                max_lr=0.40,
                beta_frac=0.30,
                beta_frac_min=0.02,
                beta_decay=0.998,
                no_imp_limit=300,
                noise_scale=0.18,
                momentum=0.35,
                tag=f"alt_n{n_alt}_{idx}"
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc_alt) and sc_alt < best_score:
                best_p = p_alt
                best_score = sc_alt
                print(f"  alt improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")
                break

    # ---------------- Stage 5: final polish ----------------
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if best_p.size <= 2048:
            lp_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(0.0, min(60.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35)))
            if time.monotonic() < lp_end:
                pl, scl = _lp_refine_full(best_p, lp_end, t=0.20, max_solves=5, tag="final_lp_full")
                if np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
                    best_p = pl
                    best_score = scl
                    print(f"  final LP improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
            if best_p.size <= 2048:
                pf, scf = _gradient_search(
                    best_p,
                    deadline,
                    rng,
                    lr=0.04,
                    max_lr=0.18,
                    beta_frac=0.08,
                    beta_frac_min=0.01,
                    beta_decay=0.999,
                    no_imp_limit=350,
                    noise_scale=0.03,
                    momentum=0.0,
                    tag="final_small"
                )
            else:
                pf, scf = _gradient_search(
                    best_p,
                    deadline,
                    rng,
                    lr=0.06,
                    max_lr=0.25,
                    beta_frac=0.10,
                    beta_frac_min=0.015,
                    beta_decay=0.999,
                    no_imp_limit=500,
                    noise_scale=0.04,
                    momentum=0.20,
                    tag="final_large"
                )
            if np.isfinite(scf) and scf < best_score:
                best_p = pf
                best_score = scf
                print(f"  final polish improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

        # Last tiny LP push if size permits and time remains.
        if best_p.size <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10:
            pl, scl = _lp_refine_active(
                best_p,
                deadline,
                rng,
                top_k=max(128, min(512, best_p.size // 8)),
                t=0.15,
                max_solves=3,
                tag="final_lp_active"
            )
            if np.isfinite(scl) and scl < best_score:
                best_p = pl
                best_score = scl
                print(f"  final active LP improved: n={best_p.size} score={best_score:.10f}")

    final_clean = _normalize_nonneg(best_p)
    final_internal = _score_norm(final_clean)

    # If the parent is clearly better internally, prefer it.
    if parent is not None:
        parent_clean = _normalize_nonneg(parent_p)
        parent_internal = _score_norm(parent_clean)
        if np.isfinite(parent_internal) and parent_internal < final_internal:
            final_clean = parent_clean
            final_internal = parent_internal

    # Optional sanity evaluation, choosing the better of final and parent if available.
    chosen_seq = final_clean
    chosen_eval = final_internal
    try:
        ev_final = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final_clean]))
        if np.isfinite(ev_final):
            chosen_eval = ev_final
            chosen_seq = final_clean

        if parent is not None and parent_clean.size != final_clean.size:
            ev_parent = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in parent_clean]))
            if np.isfinite(ev_parent) and ev_parent < chosen_eval:
                chosen_seq = parent_clean
                chosen_eval = ev_parent
    except Exception:
        pass

    chosen_seq = _normalize_nonneg(chosen_seq)
    print(f"FINAL internal: n={chosen_seq.size} score {chosen_eval:.10f}")
    return [float(x) for x in chosen_seq]
```
